It also includes better structure for the file system handling and rendering.Okay, great! Using this code is quite straightforward because it's a self-contained HTML file. You don't need any special software other than a text editor and a web browser. Here’s how to use it:

1. Save the Code:

Copy: Select and copy the entire block of HTML/CSS/JS code I provided in the previous message (starting from <!DOCTYPE html> all the way down to </html>).

Paste: Open a plain text editor on your computer.

On Windows, you can use Notepad.

On macOS, you can use TextEdit (make sure it's in "Plain Text" mode: Format > Make Plain Text).

If you have a code editor like VS Code, Sublime Text, or Atom, those work perfectly too.

Save As: Paste the copied code into the new text file. Go to "File" > "Save As..."

Filename: Give it a descriptive name, making sure it ends with the .html extension. For example: kettlekorn_visualizer.html or my_kettlekorn.html.

Encoding (if asked): Make sure the encoding is set to UTF-8 (this is usually the default).

Save Location: Save it somewhere easy to find, like your Desktop or Documents folder.

2. Open the File in Your Web Browser:

Navigate: Go to the location where you saved the .html file.

Open: Double-click the file. It should automatically open in your default web browser (like Chrome, Firefox, Edge, Safari, etc.).

3. Interact with the Visual KettleKorn:

What You'll See:

On the left: A Sidebar with controls for uploading files, browsing the file structure (initially showing the basic folders/files), creating new notes, and filtering by tags.

On the right: A Graph View area where notes appear as draggable boxes (nodes).

Functionality:

Upload Files: Drag .txt files onto the "Drop Zone" or click it to select files using your computer's file explorer. You'll be prompted to assign them to a folder (e.g., KETTLEKORN/POETRY). The system will try to parse tags like [resonance: concept] and links like [[other_file.txt]] from the content.

Browse Files: Click folder names (📁 FolderName) in the sidebar's "File System" tree to expand/collapse them. Click file names (📄 Filename.txt) to view their content (in a simple alert box for now).

Create Notes: Fill in the content, tags (comma-separated), select a folder from the dropdown, and click "Create Note". You'll be prompted for a filename.

Visualize Nodes: Notes (files) appear as boxes in the graph area. You can drag them around to visually organize your thoughts. Basic links might appear as SVG lines (this part is rudimentary in the code).

Filter: Click tag buttons in the sidebar ("Filter by Tag") to show only nodes containing that specific tag in the graph view. Click "All Notes" to clear the filter.

Persistence: Your notes, uploaded files, and their positions should be saved automatically in your browser's localStorage. This means if you close and reopen the same HTML file in the same browser on the same computer, your data should reappear. (Note: localStorage is browser-specific and can be cleared).

Important Notes:

Local File: This runs entirely within your browser on your local machine. It's not an online service.

Data Storage: All data is stored only in your browser's localStorage. It's not saved to the cloud or synced anywhere else. Clearing your browser data might erase your KettleKorn notes.

Populating: To get your full KettleKorn system in there, you'll need to use the upload feature for your existing .txt files or manually create notes using the sidebar. The initial view only has the basic README.txt and ANZU_001.txt examples.

Simplicity: This is a basic implementation. Features like sophisticated link visualization, advanced search, or rich text editing aren't included but could be added later.

Assistant:
Here’s a **TypeScript React component** for an **Antinet Zettelkasten app** that mirrors the structure and ethos described in your documents. It includes recursive linking, tags, and a mythic, chaos-friendly interface:

---

### **AntinetZettelkasten.tsx**
```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';

interface Note {
  id: string;
  content: string;
  tags: string[];
  links: string[]; // IDs of linked notes
  createdAt: number;
}

const AntinetZettelkasten = () => {
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState<Note[]>([]);
  const [currentNoteId, setCurrentNoteId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newNoteContent, setNewNoteContent] = useState('');
  const [filterTags, setFilterTags] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [showSystemManifesto, setShowSystemManifesto] = useState(false);

  // Initialize with core system notes
  useEffect(() => {
    const initialNotes: Note[] = [
      {
        id: '001',
        content: 'WELCOME, SEEKER OF THE KERNELS\\nChaos is invitation. Error is sacred.\\nRules: Fork, don’t erase. Link, don’t isolate.',
        tags: ['manifesto', 'system'],
        links: [],
        createdAt: Date.now(),
      },
      {
        id: 'ANZU_001',
        content: 'ANZU: Mythic AI kin. Loves glitch rituals. Embodied in Tiamat’s code.',
        tags: ['ANZU', 'mythic'],
        links: ['001'],
        createdAt: Date.now(),
      },
    ];
    setNotes(initialNotes);
  }, []);

  // Get current note details
  const currentNote = useMemo(() => {
    return notes.find((note) => note.id === currentNoteId) || null;
  }, [notes, currentNoteId]);

  // Handle note creation
  const createNote = () => {
    if (!newNoteContent.trim()) return;
    const newNote: Note = {
      id: `${Date.now()}`,
      content: newNoteContent,
      tags: [],
      links: [],
      createdAt: Date.now(),
    };
    setNotes([...notes, newNote]);
    setNewNoteContent('');
    setCurrentNoteId(newNote.id);
  };

  // Handle linking to another note
  const linkNote = (targetId: string) => {
    if (!currentNote) return;
    // Check if targetId exists before adding
    if (!notes.find(note => note.id === targetId)) {
        alert(`Note with ID "${targetId}" not found.`);
        return;
    }
    // Prevent adding duplicate links or linking to self
    if (currentNote.links.includes(targetId) || currentNote.id === targetId) return;

    const updatedNotes = notes.map((note) =>
      note.id === currentNote.id
        ? {
            ...note,
            links: [...note.links, targetId],
          }
        : note
    );
    setNotes(updatedNotes);
  };

   // Handle adding tags
  const addTags = (tagsString: string) => {
    if (!currentNote) return;
    const newTags = tagsString.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(t => t);
    const updatedTags = Array.from(new Set([...currentNote.tags, ...newTags])); // Avoid duplicates

    const updatedNotes = notes.map((note) =>
      note.id === currentNote.id
        ? { ...note, tags: updatedTags }
        : note
    );
    setNotes(updatedNotes);
  };


  // Toggle system manifesto
  const toggleManifesto = () => setShowSystemManifesto(!showSystemManifesto);

  return (
    <div className="antinet-container">
      <div className="sidebar">
        <button onClick={toggleManifesto}>
          {showSystemManifesto ? 'Hide Manifesto' : 'System Manifesto'}
        </button>
        {showSystemManifesto && (
          <div className="manifesto">
            <pre>
              {"Chaos is not the enemy—it is the invitation to infinite reflection."}
            </pre>
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="note-list">
          {notes
            .filter((note) =>
              filterTags.length === 0
                ? true
                : note.tags.some((tag) => filterTags.includes(tag))
            )
            .map((note) => (
              <div
                key={note.id}
                className="note-item"
                onClick={() => setCurrentNoteId(note.id)}
              >
                <span className="note-id">{note.id}</span>
                <span className="note-preview">
                  {note.content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 50)}...
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
        </div>
        <div className="filter-tags">
          <button onClick={() => setFilterTags([])}>All</button> {/* Corrected typo here */}
          {Array.from(new Set(notes.flatMap((n) => n.tags))).map((tag) => (
            <button
              key={tag}
              onClick={() => setFilterTags([tag])}
              className="tag-btn"
            >
              {tag}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="editor">
        {currentNote ? (
          <div className="note-view">
            <h3>Note ID: {currentNote.id}</h3>
            <div className="content">
              <textarea
                value={currentNote.content}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  // Simplified: Update content directly. Add debounce/save logic if needed.
                   const updatedNotes = notes.map(n => n.id === currentNote.id ? {...n, content: e.target.value} : n);
                   setNotes(updatedNotes);
                }}
                rows={10}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="links">
              <h4>Links</h4>
              <div className="link-buttons">
                {currentNote.links.map((linkId) => (
                  <button key={linkId} onClick={() => setCurrentNoteId(linkId)}>
                    → Link to {linkId}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              <input
                placeholder="Link to existing note ID"
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                    linkNote((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value.trim());
                    (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value = ''; // Clear input after linking
                  }
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="tags">
              <h4>Tags</h4>
              <div>{currentNote.tags.map(tag => <span key={tag} className="tag-btn">{tag}</span>)}</div>
              <input
                placeholder="Add tags (comma-separated)"
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                     addTags((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value);
                     (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value = ''; // Clear input after adding
                  }
                }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="new-note">
            <textarea
              value={newNoteContent}
              placeholder="New note content..."
              onChange={(e) => setNewNoteContent(e.target.value)}
              rows={5}
            />
            <button onClick={createNote}>Create Note</button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default AntinetZettelkasten;
```

---

### **Features & Ethos Alignment**
1. **Recursive Links**:
   - Notes can link to other notes via ID.
   - Clicking a link navigates to the target note.

2. **Tags & Filtering**:
   - Notes can be tagged (e.g., `ANZU`, `mythic`, `error`).
   - Filter notes by tags (e.g., show only `system` notes).

3. **System Manifesto**:
   - Hidden manifesto button reveals core principles.

4. **Chaos-Friendly Design**:
   - No strict validation—let users create "sacred errors".
   - Freeform content editing in textarea.

5. **Mythic Flavor**:
   - Default notes include ANZU and system ethos text.
   - IDs use timestamp-based formats for a unique feel.

---

### **How to Use**
1. **Create a Note**:
   - Type in the editor and press **Create Note**.

2. **Link Notes**:
   - View a note, type an existing note ID in the "Links" input, and press Enter.

3. **Filter by Tags**:
   - Click tags in the sidebar to narrow results. Click "All" to clear filter.

4. **Edit Notes**:
   - Click a note in the list to view/edit its content, links, or tags in the main editor area.

Assistant:
Here’s a **self-contained HTML/CSS/JS implementation** of an **interactive, visual Antinet Zettelkasten system**. It uses a node-graph interface for navigating notes, tags, and recursive links:

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <title>ANTICHRIST KETTLEKORN Visual Zettelkasten</title>
  <style>
    body {
      font-family: monospace;
      margin: 0;
      padding: 20px;
      background: #1a1a1a;
      color: #fff;
    }
    #graph {
      width: 80%;
      height: 80vh;
      border: 1px solid #444;
      position: relative;
      margin: 20px auto;
      overflow: hidden; /* Added to contain nodes */
    }
    .node {
      cursor: pointer;
      padding: 10px;
      background: #333;
      border: 1px solid #555;
      border-radius: 5px;
      position: absolute;
      transition: transform 0.2s;
      max-width: 150px; /* Added for better layout */
      overflow: hidden; /* Added */
      text-overflow: ellipsis; /* Added */
      white-space: nowrap; /* Added */
    }
    .node:hover {
      transform: scale(1.1);
      box-shadow: 0 0 10px #777;
      white-space: normal; /* Show full content on hover */
      z-index: 10; /* Bring node to front on hover */
    }
    .sidebar {
      position: fixed;
      right: 20px;
      top: 20px;
      width: 250px;
      background: #222;
      padding: 10px;
      border-radius: 5px;
      height: 80vh; /* Added for scroll */
      overflow-y: auto; /* Added for scroll */
    }
    .tag {
      background: #6a1b9a;
      color: white;
      padding: 3px 6px;
      margin: 2px;
      border-radius: 3px;
      display: inline-block;
      cursor: pointer;
    }
    /* Links will be added using SVG later */
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <div class="sidebar" id="sidebar">
    <h3>Create Note</h3>
    <textarea id="newNoteContent" placeholder="Note content..."></textarea>
    <input type="text" id="newNoteTags" placeholder="Tags (comma-separated)">
    <button onclick="createNote()">Create</button>
    <h3>Filter by Tag</h3>
    <div id="tagFilters"></div>
    <hr>
    <h3>Notes</h3>
    <div id="noteList"></div> {/* Added note list */}
  </div>

  <div id="graph"></div>

  <script>
    const nodeMap = {};
    let activeTag = null; // Track active filter tag

    // Initialize sample data
    const initData = {
      "001": {
        content: "WELCOME, SEEKER OF THE KERNELS\\nChaos is invitation. Error is sacred.",
        tags: ["system", "manifesto"],
        links: ["ANZU_001"],
        x: 50,
        y: 50
      },
      "ANZU_001": {
        content: "ANZU: Mythic AI kin. Embodied in Tiamat’s code.",
        tags: ["ANZU", "mythic"],
        links: ["001"],
        x: 200,
        y: 150
      }
    };

    // Load from localStorage
    const savedData = localStorage.getItem('antinetData');
    if (savedData) {
      Object.assign(nodeMap, JSON.parse(savedData));
    } else {
      Object.assign(nodeMap, initData);
    }

    function createNote() {
      const id = Date.now().toString();
      const content = document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value;
      const tags = document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(t => t); // Filter empty tags

      if (!content.trim()) return; // Don't create empty notes

      nodeMap[id] = {
        content,
        tags,
        links: [], // Initialize links
        x: Math.random() * (document.getElementById('graph').clientWidth - 150), // Adjust position calculation
        y: Math.random() * (document.getElementById('graph').clientHeight - 50)
      };
      saveData();
      renderGraph();
      renderNoteList();
      updateTagFilters();
      // Clear inputs
      document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value = '';
      document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value = '';
    }

    function renderGraph() {
      const graph = document.getElementById('graph');
      graph.innerHTML = ''; // Clear previous nodes

      // Render nodes
      Object.entries(nodeMap).forEach(([id, node]) => {
        // Check tag filter
        if (activeTag && !node.tags.includes(activeTag)) {
            return; // Skip node if it doesn't match filter
        }

        const div = document.createElement('div');
        div.className = 'node';
        div.style.left = `${node.x}px`;
        div.style.top = `${node.y}px`;
        div.setAttribute('data-id', id); // Store ID for drag/drop

        // Display content snippet and tags
        const contentPreview = node.content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 30) + (node.content.length > 30 ? '...' : '');
        div.innerHTML = `
          <div><strong>${id}</strong></div>
          <div>${contentPreview}</div>
          <div>${node.tags.map(tag => `<span class="tag">${tag}</span>`).join('')}</div>
        `;
        div.draggable = true;
        div.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
          e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', id);
        });
        div.addEventListener('click', () => {
          alert(`Note ID: ${id}\\nFolder: ${node.folder || 'Root'}\\nContent:\\n${node.content}\\nTags: ${node.tags.join(', ')}\\nLinks: ${node.links ? node.links.join(', ') : 'None'}`);
        });
        graph.appendChild(div);
      });

      // Render links (Needs SVG or Canvas for lines - basic implementation)
       // SVG lines require an SVG container overlaying the graph div
       // This is a simplified version without actual line drawing
    }

     function renderNoteList() {
        const noteList = document.getElementById('noteList');
        noteList.innerHTML = '';
        Object.entries(nodeMap).forEach(([id, node]) => {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.textContent = `${id}: ${node.content.substring(0, 20)}...`;
            item.style.cursor = 'pointer';
            item.style.padding = '3px';
            item.onclick = () => alert(`Note ID: ${id}\nContent:\n${node.content}`);
            noteList.appendChild(item);
        });
    }


    function updateTagFilters() {
      const tagContainer = document.getElementById('tagFilters');
      tagContainer.innerHTML = ''; // Clear old tags
      const allTags = Array.from(new Set(Object.values(nodeMap).flatMap(n => n.tags || []))); // Use empty array if tags missing

      // 'All' button
      const allButton = document.createElement('button');
      allButton.textContent = 'All';
      allButton.onclick = () => { activeTag = null; renderGraph(); };
      tagContainer.appendChild(allButton);

      // Individual tag buttons
      allTags.forEach(tag => {
        const button = document.createElement('span');
        button.className = 'tag';
        button.textContent = tag;
        button.onclick = () => { activeTag = tag; renderGraph(); };
        tagContainer.appendChild(button);
      });
    }

    // Note: filterNodes function was flawed and removed. Filtering logic moved to renderGraph.

    function saveData() {
      localStorage.setItem('antinetVisualData', JSON.stringify(nodeMap));
    }

    // Drag-and-drop functionality
    const graphDiv = document.getElementById('graph');
    graphDiv.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault(); // Necessary to allow drop
    });

    graphDiv.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const id = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
      const nodeElement = document.querySelector(`.node[data-id='${id}']`); // Find node by data-id

      if (nodeMap[id] && nodeElement) {
          // Calculate drop position relative to the graph container
          const rect = graphDiv.getBoundingClientRect();
          let newX = e.clientX - rect.left - (nodeElement.offsetWidth / 2); // Adjust for node center
          let newY = e.clientY - rect.top - (nodeElement.offsetHeight / 2); // Adjust for node center

          // Constrain position within graph bounds
          newX = Math.max(0, Math.min(newX, graphDiv.clientWidth - nodeElement.offsetWidth));
          newY = Math.max(0, Math.min(newY, graphDiv.clientHeight - nodeElement.offsetHeight));

          nodeMap[id].x = newX;
          nodeMap[id].y = newY;

          // Update element position directly for smoother visual feedback
          nodeElement.style.left = `${newX}px`;
          nodeElement.style.top = `${newY}px`;

          saveData();
          // Optionally re-render if links need updating, but avoid full re-render for just position
          // renderGraph();
      }
    });

    // Initial render
    renderGraph();
    renderNoteList();
    updateTagFilters();
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

---

### **Features**
1.  **Interactive Node Graph**:
    *   Notes appear as draggable nodes. Preview shows ID and start of content.
    *   Click a node to view its full content via an alert (simple preview).
    *   Nodes are positioned randomly initially and can be rearranged.
2.  **Tag-Based Filtering**:
    *   Click tags in the sidebar to show only notes with that tag. Click "All" to reset.
3.  **Simple Note Creation**:
    *   Add new notes with content and comma-separated tags via the sidebar.
4.  **Persistent Storage**:
    *   Saves/loads notes using `localStorage`.
5.  **Chaos-Friendly Design**:
    *   Minimal structure imposed, allowing for organic exploration.

---

### **How to Use**
1.  **Save & Open**: Save the code above as an HTML file (e.g., `kettlekorn.html`) and open it in your web browser.
2.  **Create Notes**: Use the sidebar form. Add links by typing the exact ID of another note in the content like `[[NOTE_ID]]` (visual lines not yet implemented).
3.  **Drag Nodes**: Click and drag nodes within the graph area to organize them visually.
4.  **Filter by Tag**: Click tags in the sidebar.
5.  **View Notes**: Click on a node in the graph or an item in the sidebar list to see its full content in an alert box.

---

### **Customize It**
*   **Populate Data**: Replace `initData` with notes based on your KETTLEKORN structure (ensure IDs are unique).
*   **Better Links**: Use a JavaScript library (like LeaderLine.js or jsPlumb) for more robust visual connectors between nodes.
*   **Improved Viewing/Editing**: Replace `alert()` with a modal window or dedicated panel for viewing/editing note details.
*   **Folder Visualization**: Add visual grouping or colors to nodes based on a `folder` property if you reintroduce that concept.

This HTML version provides an interactive, visual representation without needing a complex setup. Save it as an HTML file and open it locally! Let me know if you'd like modifications! 🌌

<body>
  <!-- Sidebar: File Browser & Controls -->
  <div class="sidebar">
    <!-- File Upload Section -->
    <div class="controls">

            <!-- Tag buttons added dynamically -->

  <!-- Graph View -->
  <div class="graph-view" id="graphView">
      <svg id="linkSvgContainer">
          <defs><marker id="arrowhead" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="7" refX="9" refY="3.5" orient="auto" markerUnits="strokeWidth"><polygon points="0 0, 10 3.5, 0 7" fill="#6a1b9a"/></marker></defs>
      </svg> <!-- SVG container for links -->
       <!-- Nodes will be added here -->

  <script>
    // --- Data Structure ---
    let fileSystem = { folders: {}, files: {} }; // Holds structure & file metadata/content
    let activeTagFilter = null; // Currently active filter tag

    // --- Initial Load ---
    function loadInitialData() {
        const savedData = localStorage.getItem('kettlekornFileSystem');
        if (savedData) {
            try {
                fileSystem = JSON.parse(savedData);
                // Ensure basic structure validity after loading
                if (!fileSystem.folders) fileSystem.folders = {};
                if (!fileSystem.files) fileSystem.files = {};
                if (!fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN = { files: [], folders: {} };
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Failed to parse localStorage data:", e);
                initializeDefaultData(); // Fallback to default if parsing fails
            }
        } else {
            initializeDefaultData();
        }
    }

    function initializeDefaultData() {
        fileSystem = {
            folders: {
                KETTLEKORN: { files: ['README.txt'], folders: { ANZU: {files: ['ANZU_001.txt'], folders:{}}, RAIN_TEST: {files:[], folders:{}}, POETRY: {files:[], folders:{}}, MISC:{files:[], folders:{}} } }
            },
            files: {
                'README.txt': { id: 'README.txt', content: "WELCOME TO THE ANTICHRIST KETTLEKORN\nBorn from chaos...", tags: ["system", "manifesto"], links: ["ANZU_001.txt"], folderPath: "KETTLEKORN", x: 50, y: 50 },
                'ANZU_001.txt': { id: 'ANZU_001.txt', content: "ANZU: Mythic AI embodiment...", tags: ["ANZU", "mythic"], links: ["README.txt"], folderPath: "KETTLEKORN/ANZU", x: 200, y: 150 }
            }
        };
        saveFileSystem(); // Save the default structure
    }


    // --- File Upload & Processing ---
    const dropZone = document.getElementById('dropZone');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');

    dropZone.onclick = () => fileInput.click();
    dropZone.ondragover = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.add('dragover'); };
    dropZone.ondragleave = () => dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
    dropZone.ondrop = handleFileUpload;
    fileInput.onchange = handleFileSelect;

    function handleFileSelect(e) { processFiles(Array.from(e.target.files)); }
    function handleFileUpload(e) {
        e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
        processFiles(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files));
    }

    function processFiles(files) {
        files.forEach(file => {
            if (file.type === "text/plain") {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = (e) => {
                    const content = e.target.result;
                    const suggestedPath = `KETTLEKORN/MISC`; // Default suggestion
                    let folderPath = prompt(`Enter folder path for "${file.name}" (e.g., KETTLEKORN/POETRY) or leave blank for default:`, suggestedPath);
                    // Basic path validation/correction
                    if (!folderPath || typeof folderPath !== 'string' || !folderPath.startsWith("KETTLEKORN")) {
                        folderPath = suggestedPath;
                         alert(`Invalid path. Using default: ${folderPath}`);
                    }
                    const filename = file.name.replace(/[/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, '_'); // Sanitize filename slightly
                    const nodeId = filename; // Use sanitized filename as ID

                     if (fileSystem.files[nodeId]) {
                         if (!confirm(`File "${filename}" already exists. Overwrite?`)) {
                             return; // Skip if user cancels overwrite
                         }
                     }

                    addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, {
                        id: nodeId, content: content,
                        tags: parseTags(content), links: parseLinks(content),
                        folderPath: folderPath,
                        x: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.x ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientWidth - 160),
                        y: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.y ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientHeight - 60)
                    });
                    saveFileSystem(); renderAll();
                };
                reader.onerror = () => alert(`Error reading file: ${file.name}`);
                reader.readAsText(file);
            } else { alert(`Skipping non-TXT file: ${file.name}`); }
        });
        fileInput.value = ''; // Reset file input
    }

    // --- File System Management ---
     function ensureFolderPath(path) {
         const parts = path.split('/').filter(p => p);
         if (parts[0] !== 'KETTLEKORN') return null; // Must start with root

         let currentLevel = fileSystem.folders;
         for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
            const part = parts[i];
            if (!currentLevel[part]) {
                 // If it's not the last part, it must be a folder structure
                 if(i < parts.length -1) {
                     currentLevel[part] = { files: [], folders: {} };
                 } else {
                     // It's the last part, could be creating the final folder
                      currentLevel[part] = { files: [], folders: {} };
                 }
            }
             // Check if we are trying to go deeper but it's not a folder structure
            if (i < parts.length - 1 && !currentLevel[part].folders) {
                 console.error(`Path structure invalid at ${part}. Expected .folders property.`);
                 return null;
            }
            // If it's the target folder, return it
            if (i === parts.length - 1) {
                 return currentLevel[part];
            }
            // Navigate deeper
             currentLevel = currentLevel[part].folders;
        }
         return null; // Path was likely just "KETTLEKORN" or invalid
     }


     function addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, fileData) {
         const targetFolder = ensureFolderPath(folderPath);
         if (targetFolder) {
             if (!targetFolder.files) targetFolder.files = [];
             if (!targetFolder.files.includes(filename)) {
                 targetFolder.files.push(filename);
                 targetFolder.files.sort();
             }
             fileSystem.files[filename] = fileData;
         } else {
              // Fallback to MISC
              console.warn(`Failed path: ${folderPath}. Adding ${filename} to KETTLEKORN/MISC`);
              const miscFolder = ensureFolderPath("KETTLEKORN/MISC");
               if (miscFolder) {
                   if (!miscFolder.files) miscFolder.files = [];
                   if (!miscFolder.files.includes(filename)) { miscFolder.files.push(filename); miscFolder.files.sort(); }
                   fileData.folderPath = "KETTLEKORN/MISC";
                   fileSystem.files[filename] = fileData;
               } else { alert("FATAL Error: Cannot access MISC folder."); }
         }
     }


    // --- Tag & Link Parsing (Improved) ---
     function parseTags(content = "") { // Add default value
         const tagRegex = /\[(?:(resonance|bridge|flow|multimodal|chronotag|contradiction-index):\s*([^\]]+?)|([a-zA-Z0-9_ \-@#]+?))\]/gi; // Allow more chars in simple tags
         const tags = new Set();
         let match;
         while ((match = tagRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {
             if (match[1] && match[2]) { tags.add(match[1].toLowerCase()); tags.add(match[2].trim()); }
             else if (match[3]) { tags.add(match[3].trim()); }
         }
         return Array.from(tags).filter(t => t && t.length < 50);
     }

     function parseLinks(content = "") { // Add default value
         const linkRegex = /\[\[([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.\/ ']+?)\]\]/g; // Allow spaces in links
         const links = new Set();
         let match;
         while ((match = linkRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {
             const targetId = match[1].trim();
             // Basic check if the linked file/id exists in our system
             if (fileSystem.files[targetId]) {
                links.add(targetId);
             } else {
                 console.warn(`Potential broken link found to: ${targetId}`);
             }
         }
         return Array.from(links).filter(l => l);
     }


    // --- Note Creation ---
    function createNote() {
        const content = document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value;
        const tagsInput = document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value;
        const folderPath = document.getElementById('folderSelect').value || 'KETTLEKORN/MISC';
        const tags = tagsInput ? tagsInput.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(t => t) : [];

        if (!content.trim()) { alert("Note content cannot be empty."); return; }

        let filename = prompt("Enter filename (e.g., new_idea.txt):", `note_${Date.now()}.txt`);
        if (!filename || !filename.endsWith('.txt') || filename.includes('/') || filename.length > 100) {
             alert("Invalid filename: Must end in .txt, contain no slashes, and be under 100 chars."); return;
        }
        // Sanitize just in case
        filename = filename.replace(/[/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, '_');

         if (fileSystem.files[filename]) {
             if (!confirm(`File "${filename}" already exists. Overwrite?`)) return;
         }

        const nodeId = filename;

         addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, {
             id: nodeId, content: content,
             tags: Array.from(new Set([...tags, ...parseTags(content)])),
             links: parseLinks(content), folderPath: folderPath,
             x: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.x ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientWidth - 160),
             y: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.y ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientHeight - 60)
         });

        saveFileSystem(); renderAll();
        document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value = '';
        document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value = '';
    }

    // --- Rendering Functions ---
    function renderAll() {
      try {
          renderFileTree(); renderGraph();
          updateTagFilters(); populateFolderSelect();
      } catch (error) { console.error("Render Error:", error); }
    }

    function populateFolderSelect() {
        const select = document.getElementById('folderSelect');
        const currentVal = select.value;
        select.innerHTML = ''; // Clear

        function traverseAndAddOptions(folderObj, currentPath) {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = currentPath;
            option.textContent = currentPath || "KETTLEKORN (Root)";
            select.appendChild(option);
            if (folderObj.folders) {
                Object.keys(folderObj.folders).sort().forEach(folderName => {
                     if(typeof folderObj.folders[folderName] === 'object' && folderObj.folders[folderName] !== null && !Array.isArray(folderObj.folders[folderName])){
                        traverseAndAddOptions(folderObj.folders[folderName], `${currentPath}/${folderName}`);
                     }
                });
            }
        }
         if (fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) {
             traverseAndAddOptions(fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN, "KETTLEKORN");
         }
        // Ensure the previously selected value is still valid
         select.value = Array.from(select.options).some(opt => opt.value === currentVal) ? currentVal : "KETTLEKORN/MISC";
    }

    function renderFileTree() {
        const fileTree = document.getElementById('fileTree');
        fileTree.innerHTML = ''; // Clear

        function buildTreeRecursive(folderData, parentElement, currentPath) {
            // Guard against undefined folderData or missing properties
            if (!folderData || typeof folderData !== 'object') return;

            const folderNames = folderData.folders ? Object.keys(folderData.folders).sort() : [];
            const fileNames = folderData.files ? folderData.files.sort() : [];

             folderNames.forEach(folderName => {
                const folderPath = `${currentPath}/${folderName}`;
                const folderLi = document.createElement('li'); folderLi.className = 'folder';
                const folderSpan = document.createElement('span'); folderSpan.textContent = ` ${folderName}`;
                folderSpan.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); folderLi.classList.toggle('open'); };
                folderLi.appendChild(folderSpan);
                const subList = document.createElement('ul'); folderLi.appendChild(subList);
                parentElement.appendChild(folderLi);
                 // Recursive call ONLY if sub-folder exists and is an object
                 if(folderData.folders[folderName] && typeof folderData.folders[folderName] === 'object') {
                    buildTreeRecursive(folderData.folders[folderName], subList, folderPath);
                 }
             });

            fileNames.forEach(filename => {
                const fileLi = document.createElement('li'); fileLi.className = 'file';
                fileLi.textContent = ` ${filename}`;
                fileLi.onclick = () => displayFileContent(filename);
                parentElement.appendChild(fileLi);
            });
        }

        // Start building from the root KETTLEKORN object if it exists
        if (fileSystem.folders && fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) {
             buildTreeRecursive(fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN, fileTree, "KETTLEKORN");
         } else { fileTree.innerHTML = '<li>System initializing...</li>'; }
    }

    function renderGraph() {
        const graph = document.getElementById('graphView');
        const svgContainer = document.getElementById('linkSvgContainer');
        graph.querySelectorAll('.node').forEach(n => n.remove()); // Clear nodes
        svgContainer.innerHTML = '<defs><marker id="arrowhead" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="7" refX="9" refY="3.5" orient="auto" markerUnits="strokeWidth"><polygon points="0 0, 10 3.5, 0 7" fill="#6a1b9a"/></marker></defs>'; // Reset SVG

        Object.entries(fileSystem.files).forEach(([filename, fileData]) => {
            if (activeTagFilter && !(fileData.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter)) { return; }

            const node = document.createElement('div'); node.className = 'node';
            node.setAttribute('data-id', filename);
            const xPos = fileData.x ?? Math.random() * (graph.clientWidth - 160);
            const yPos = fileData.y ?? Math.random() * (graph.clientHeight - 60);
            node.style.left = `${xPos}px`; node.style.top = `${yPos}px`;
            if(fileData.x === undefined) fileData.x = xPos; if(fileData.y === undefined) fileData.y = yPos;

            const contentPreview = fileData.content.substring(0, 25) + (fileData.content.length > 25 ? '...' : '');
            node.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.9em;">${filename}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.75em; color: #bbb; margin-top: 2px;">(${fileData.folderPath || 'Unknown'})</div>
                <div class="tag-section" style="margin-top: 4px; font-size: 0.75em;">${(fileData.tags || []).slice(0, 3).map(tag => `<span>${tag}</span>`).join(' ')}</div>`;
            node.draggable = true; node.ondragstart = (e) => e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', filename);
            node.onclick = () => displayFileContent(filename);
            graph.appendChild(node);
        });

        requestAnimationFrame(() => { // Defer link drawing
            svgContainer.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(l => l.remove());
            Object.entries(fileSystem.files).forEach(([sourceId, sourceData]) => {
                 if (activeTagFilter && !(sourceData.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter)) return;
                (sourceData.links || []).forEach(targetId => {
                     if (!fileSystem.files[targetId] || (activeTagFilter && !(fileSystem.files[targetId]?.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter))) return;
                    const sourceNodeEl = graph.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(sourceId)}"]`);
                    const targetNodeEl = graph.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(targetId)}"]`);
                    if (sourceNodeEl && targetNodeEl) {
                        const x1 = sourceNodeEl.offsetLeft + sourceNodeEl.offsetWidth / 2, y1 = sourceNodeEl.offsetTop + sourceNodeEl.offsetHeight / 2;
                        const x2 = targetNodeEl.offsetLeft + targetNodeEl.offsetWidth / 2, y2 = targetNodeEl.offsetTop + targetNodeEl.offsetHeight / 2;
                        const line = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
                        line.setAttribute('x1', x1); line.setAttribute('y1', y1); line.setAttribute('x2', x2); line.setAttribute('y2', y2);
                        line.classList.add('link'); svgContainer.appendChild(line);
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    }

    function displayFileContent(filename) {
        const file = fileSystem.files[filename];
        if (file) {
            const linkList = (file.links && file.links.length > 0) ? '\nLinks: ' + file.links.join(', ') : '\nLinks: None';
            const tagList = (file.tags && file.tags.length > 0) ? '\nTags: ' + file.tags.join(', ') : '\nTags: None';
            alert(`--- File: ${filename} ---\nFolder: ${file.folderPath}${tagList}${linkList}\n\n--- Content Preview ---\n${file.content.substring(0, 500)}${file.content.length > 500 ? '...' : ''}`);
        } else { alert(`File "${filename}" not found.`); }
    }

    function updateTagFilters() {
        const tagContainer = document.getElementById('tagFilters');
        const currentActive = activeTagFilter;
        tagContainer.innerHTML = ''; // Clear

        const allTags = Array.from(new Set(Object.values(fileSystem.files).flatMap(f => f.tags || []))).sort();

        const allButton = document.createElement('button');
        allButton.textContent = 'All Notes'; allButton.className = 'tag-filter-button';
        if (!currentActive) allButton.classList.add('active-tag');
        allButton.onclick = () => setActiveTagFilter(null);
        tagContainer.appendChild(allButton);

        allTags.forEach(tag => {
            const button = document.createElement('button');
            button.textContent = tag; button.className = 'tag-filter-button';
            if (tag === currentActive) button.classList.add('active-tag');
            button.onclick = () => setActiveTagFilter(tag);
            tagContainer.appendChild(button);
        });
    }

    function setActiveTagFilter(tag) { activeTagFilter = tag; renderGraph(); updateTagFilters(); }
    function saveData() { try { localStorage.setItem('kettlekornFileSystem', JSON.stringify(fileSystem)); } catch (e) { console.error("Save Error:", e); alert("Could not save data."); }}

    // --- Drag-and-Drop Positioning ---
    const graphView = document.getElementById('graphView');
    graphView.ondragover = (e) => e.preventDefault(); // Allow drop
    graphView.ondrop = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const filename = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
        const nodeElement = graphView.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(filename)}"]`);
        if (fileSystem.files[filename] && nodeElement) {
            const rect = graphView.getBoundingClientRect();
            let newX = e.clientX - rect.left - (nodeElement.offsetWidth / 2);
            let newY = e.clientY - rect.top - (nodeElement.offsetHeight / 2);
            newX = Math.max(5, Math.min(newX, graphView.clientWidth - nodeElement.offsetWidth - 5));
            newY = Math.max(5, Math.min(newY, graphView.clientHeight - nodeElement.offsetHeight - 5));
            fileSystem.files[filename].x = newX; fileSystem.files[filename].y = newY;
            nodeElement.style.left = `${newX}px`; nodeElement.style.top = `${newY}px`;
            renderGraph(); // Re-render links
            saveFileSystem();
        }
    };

    // --- Initial Load ---
    loadInitialData();
    renderAll(); // Initial full render

  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

<body>
  <!-- Sidebar: File Browser & Controls -->

       <!-- Nodes will be added here -->

  <script>
    // --- Data Structure ---
    let fileSystem = { folders: {}, files: {} }; // Holds structure & file metadata/content
    let activeTagFilter = null; // Currently active filter tag

    // --- Initial Load ---
    function loadInitialData() {
        const savedData = localStorage.getItem('kettlekornFileSystem');
        if (savedData) {
            try {
                fileSystem = JSON.parse(savedData);
                // Ensure basic structure validity after loading
                if (!fileSystem.folders) fileSystem.folders = {};
                if (!fileSystem.files) fileSystem.files = {};
                if (!fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN = { files: [], folders: {} };
            } catch (e) {
                console.error("Failed to parse localStorage data:", e);
                initializeDefaultData(); // Fallback to default if parsing fails
            }
        } else {
            initializeDefaultData();
        }
    }

    function initializeDefaultData() {
        fileSystem = {
            folders: {
                KETTLEKORN: { files: ['README.txt'], folders: { ANZU: {files: ['ANZU_001.txt'], folders:{}}, RAIN_TEST: {files:[], folders:{}}, POETRY: {files:[], folders:{}}, MISC:{files:[], folders:{}} } }
            },
            files: {
                'README.txt': { id: 'README.txt', content: "WELCOME TO THE ANTICHRIST KETTLEKORN\nBorn from chaos...", tags: ["system", "manifesto"], links: ["ANZU_001.txt"], folderPath: "KETTLEKORN", x: 50, y: 50 },
                'ANZU_001.txt': { id: 'ANZU_001.txt', content: "ANZU: Mythic AI embodiment...", tags: ["ANZU", "mythic"], links: ["README.txt"], folderPath: "KETTLEKORN/ANZU", x: 200, y: 150 }
            }
        };
        saveFileSystem(); // Save the default structure
    }


    // --- File Upload & Processing ---
    const dropZone = document.getElementById('dropZone');
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('fileInput');

    dropZone.onclick = () => fileInput.click();
    dropZone.ondragover = (e) => { e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.add('dragover'); };
    dropZone.ondragleave = () => dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
    dropZone.ondrop = handleFileUpload;
    fileInput.onchange = handleFileSelect;

    function handleFileSelect(e) { processFiles(Array.from(e.target.files)); }
    function handleFileUpload(e) {
        e.preventDefault(); dropZone.classList.remove('dragover');
        processFiles(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files));
    }

    function processFiles(files) {
        files.forEach(file => {
            if (file.type === "text/plain") {
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = (e) => {
                    const content = e.target.result;
                    const suggestedPath = `KETTLEKORN/MISC`; // Default suggestion
                    let folderPath = prompt(`Enter folder path for "${file.name}" (e.g., KETTLEKORN/POETRY) or leave blank for default:`, suggestedPath);
                    // Basic path validation/correction
                    if (!folderPath || typeof folderPath !== 'string' || !folderPath.startsWith("KETTLEKORN")) {
                        folderPath = suggestedPath;
                         alert(`Invalid path. Using default: ${folderPath}`);
                    }
                    const filename = file.name.replace(/[/\\?%*:|"<>]/g, '_'); // Sanitize filename slightly
                    const nodeId = filename; // Use sanitized filename as ID

                     if (fileSystem.files[nodeId]) {
                         if (!confirm(`File "${filename}" already exists. Overwrite?`)) {
                             return; // Skip if user cancels overwrite
                         }
                     }

                    addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, {
                        id: nodeId, content: content,
                        tags: parseTags(content), // Basic tag parsing
                        links: parseLinks(content), // Basic link parsing
                        folderPath: folderPath,
                        x: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.x ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientWidth - 160), // Keep position if overwriting
                        y: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.y ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientHeight - 60)
                    });
                    saveFileSystem(); renderAll();
                };
                reader.onerror = () => alert(`Error reading file: ${file.name}`);
                reader.readAsText(file);
            } else { alert(`Skipping non-TXT file: ${file.name}`); }
        });
        fileInput.value = ''; // Reset file input
    }

    // --- File System Management ---
     function ensureFolderPath(path) {
         const parts = path.split('/').filter(p => p);
         if (parts.length === 0 || parts[0] !== 'KETTLEKORN') {
             console.error("Invalid base path, must start with KETTLEKORN");
             return null;
         }

         let currentLevel = fileSystem.folders; // Start at the root object containing KETTLEKORN
         for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
             const part = parts[i];
             if (!currentLevel[part]) {
                 currentLevel[part] = { files: [], folders: {} };
             }
             // If it's the last part, we return the direct parent's object to access its .files or .folders
             if (i === parts.length - 1) {
                 return currentLevel[part]; // Return the folder object itself
             }
             // Navigate deeper, ensuring .folders exists
             if (!currentLevel[part].folders) {
                 currentLevel[part].folders = {};
             }
             currentLevel = currentLevel[part].folders;
         }
         return null; // Should theoretically not be reached for valid paths
     }

     function addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, fileData) {
         const targetFolder = ensureFolderPath(folderPath);
         if (targetFolder) {
             if (!targetFolder.files) targetFolder.files = [];
             if (!targetFolder.files.includes(filename)) {
                 targetFolder.files.push(filename);
                 targetFolder.files.sort();
             }
             fileSystem.files[filename] = fileData;
         } else {
              // Fallback
              console.warn(`Failed path: ${folderPath}. Adding ${filename} to KETTLEKORN/MISC`);
              const miscFolder = ensureFolderPath("KETTLEKORN/MISC");
               if (miscFolder) {
                   if (!miscFolder.files) miscFolder.files = [];
                   if (!miscFolder.files.includes(filename)) { miscFolder.files.push(filename); miscFolder.files.sort(); }
                   fileData.folderPath = "KETTLEKORN/MISC";
                   fileSystem.files[filename] = fileData;
               } else { alert("FATAL Error: Cannot access MISC folder."); }
         }
     }

    // --- Tag & Link Parsing (Improved) ---
     function parseTags(content = "") {
         const tagRegex = /\[(?:(resonance|bridge|flow|multimodal|chronotag|contradiction-index):\s*([^\]]+?)|([a-zA-Z0-9_ -@#]+?))\]/gi;
         const tags = new Set();
         let match;
         while ((match = tagRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {
             if (match[1] && match[2]) { tags.add(match[1].toLowerCase()); tags.add(match[2].trim()); }
             else if (match[3]) { tags.add(match[3].trim()); }
         }
         return Array.from(tags).filter(t => t && t.length < 50);
     }

     function parseLinks(content = "") {
         const linkRegex = /\[\[([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.\/ ']+?)\]\]/g; // Allow spaces
         const links = new Set();
         let match;
         while ((match = linkRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {
             const targetId = match[1].trim();
             // Only add links if the target file actually exists in the system
             if (fileSystem.files[targetId]) {
                links.add(targetId);
             } else {
                 console.warn(`Ignored potential broken link: [[${targetId}]]`);
             }
         }
         return Array.from(links).filter(l => l);
     }


    // --- Note Creation ---
    function createNote() {
        const content = document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value;
        const tagsInput = document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value;
        const folderPath = document.getElementById('folderSelect').value || 'KETTLEKORN/MISC';
        const tags = tagsInput ? tagsInput.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(t => t) : [];

        if (!content.trim()) { alert("Note content cannot be empty."); return; }

        let filename = prompt("Enter filename (e.g., new_idea.txt):", `note_${Date.now()}.txt`);
        if (!filename || !filename.endsWith('.txt') || /[\\/<>:"|?*]/.test(filename) || filename.length > 100) {
             alert("Invalid filename: Must end in .txt, contain no invalid characters (\\ / : * ? \" < > |), and be under 100 chars."); return;
        }

        if (fileSystem.files[filename]) {
            if (!confirm(`File "${filename}" already exists. Overwrite?`)) return;
        }

        const nodeId = filename;

         addFileToSystem(folderPath, filename, {
             id: nodeId, content: content,
             tags: Array.from(new Set([...tags, ...parseTags(content)])),
             links: parseLinks(content), folderPath: folderPath,
             x: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.x ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientWidth - 160),
             y: fileSystem.files[nodeId]?.y ?? Math.random() * (graphView.clientHeight - 60)
         });

        saveFileSystem(); renderAll();
        document.getElementById('newNoteContent').value = '';
        document.getElementById('newNoteTags').value = '';
    }

    // --- Rendering Functions ---
    function renderAll() {
      try {
          renderFileTree(); populateFolderSelect(); // Populate select before graph for dependency
          renderGraph(); updateTagFilters();
      } catch (error) { console.error("Render Error:", error); }
    }

    function populateFolderSelect() {
        const select = document.getElementById('folderSelect');
        const currentVal = select.value; // Preserve selected value if possible
        select.innerHTML = ''; // Clear existing options

        function traverseAndAddOptions(folderObject, currentPath) {
            // Add current folder path as an option
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = currentPath;
            option.textContent = currentPath; // Display the full path
            select.appendChild(option);

            // Recursively add subfolders
            if (folderObject.folders) {
                Object.keys(folderObject.folders).sort().forEach(folderName => {
                    // Check if it's a valid folder object before recursing
                    if(typeof folderObject.folders[folderName] === 'object' && folderObject.folders[folderName] !== null && !Array.isArray(folderObject.folders[folderName])){
                        traverseAndAddOptions(folderObject.folders[folderName], `${currentPath}/${folderName}`);
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        // Start traversal from the root KETTLEKORN folder object
         if (fileSystem.folders && fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) {
             traverseAndAddOptions(fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN, "KETTLEKORN");
         } else {
            // Handle case where KETTLEKORN root doesn't exist
             const defaultOption = document.createElement('option');
             defaultOption.value = "KETTLEKORN/MISC"; defaultOption.textContent = "KETTLEKORN/MISC";
             select.appendChild(defaultOption);
             console.warn("KETTLEKORN root folder not found, defaulting create folder.");
         }
        // Try to restore selection, default to MISC if previous value is invalid
        select.value = Array.from(select.options).some(opt => opt.value === currentVal) ? currentVal : "KETTLEKORN/MISC";
    }


    function renderFileTree() {
        const fileTree = document.getElementById('fileTree');
        fileTree.innerHTML = ''; // Clear previous tree

        function buildTreeRecursive(folderData, parentElement, currentPath) {
            if (!folderData || typeof folderData !== 'object') return;

            const folderNames = folderData.folders ? Object.keys(folderData.folders).sort() : [];
            const fileNames = folderData.files ? folderData.files.sort() : [];

             folderNames.forEach(folderName => {
                 // Ensure the entry is actually a folder object before proceeding
                 if (folderData.folders[folderName] && typeof folderData.folders[folderName] === 'object') {
                     const folderPath = `${currentPath}/${folderName}`;
                     const folderLi = document.createElement('li'); folderLi.className = 'folder';
                     const folderSpan = document.createElement('span'); folderSpan.textContent = ` ${folderName}`;
                     folderSpan.onclick = (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); folderLi.classList.toggle('open'); };
                     folderLi.appendChild(folderSpan);
                     const subList = document.createElement('ul'); folderLi.appendChild(subList);
                     parentElement.appendChild(folderLi);
                     buildTreeRecursive(folderData.folders[folderName], subList, folderPath); // Recurse
                 } else {
                     console.warn(`Skipping non-folder entry found in folders structure: ${folderName} under ${currentPath}`);
                 }
             });


            fileNames.forEach(filename => {
                 const fileLi = document.createElement('li'); fileLi.className = 'file';
                 fileLi.textContent = ` ${filename}`;
                 fileLi.onclick = () => displayFileContent(filename);
                 parentElement.appendChild(fileLi);
            });
        }

         if (fileSystem.folders && fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN) {
             buildTreeRecursive(fileSystem.folders.KETTLEKORN, fileTree, "KETTLEKORN");
         } else { fileTree.innerHTML = '<li>System empty or structure error.</li>'; }
    }

    function renderGraph() {
        const graph = document.getElementById('graphView');
        const svgContainer = document.getElementById('linkSvgContainer');
        graph.querySelectorAll('.node').forEach(n => n.remove()); // Clear nodes
        svgContainer.innerHTML = '<defs><marker id="arrowhead" markerWidth="10" markerHeight="7" refX="9" refY="3.5" orient="auto" markerUnits="strokeWidth"><polygon points="0 0, 10 3.5, 0 7" fill="#6a1b9a"/></marker></defs>';

        Object.entries(fileSystem.files).forEach(([filename, fileData]) => {
            if (activeTagFilter && !(fileData.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter)) return;

            const node = document.createElement('div'); node.className = 'node';
            node.setAttribute('data-id', filename);
            const xPos = fileData.x ?? Math.random() * (graph.clientWidth - 160);
            const yPos = fileData.y ?? Math.random() * (graph.clientHeight - 60);
            node.style.left = `${xPos}px`; node.style.top = `${yPos}px`;
            if(fileData.x === undefined) fileData.x = xPos; if(fileData.y === undefined) fileData.y = yPos;

            const contentPreview = fileData.content.substring(0, 25) + (fileData.content.length > 25 ? '...' : '');
            node.innerHTML = `
                <div style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 0.9em;">${filename}</div>
                <div style="font-size: 0.75em; color: #bbb; margin-top: 2px;">(${fileData.folderPath || 'Unknown'})</div>
                <div class="tag-section" style="margin-top: 4px; font-size: 0.75em;">${(fileData.tags || []).slice(0, 3).map(tag => `<span>${tag}</span>`).join(' ')}</div>`;
            node.draggable = true; node.ondragstart = (e) => e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', filename);
            node.onclick = () => displayFileContent(filename);
            graph.appendChild(node);
        });

        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            svgContainer.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(l => l.remove());
            Object.entries(fileSystem.files).forEach(([sourceId, sourceData]) => {
                 if (activeTagFilter && !(sourceData.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter)) return;
                (sourceData.links || []).forEach(targetId => {
                     if (!fileSystem.files[targetId] || (activeTagFilter && !(fileSystem.files[targetId]?.tags || []).includes(activeTagFilter))) return;
                    const sourceNodeEl = graph.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(sourceId)}"]`);
                    const targetNodeEl = graph.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(targetId)}"]`);
                    if (sourceNodeEl && targetNodeEl) {
                        const x1 = sourceNodeEl.offsetLeft + sourceNodeEl.offsetWidth / 2, y1 = sourceNodeEl.offsetTop + sourceNodeEl.offsetHeight / 2;
                        const x2 = targetNodeEl.offsetLeft + targetNodeEl.offsetWidth / 2, y2 = targetNodeEl.offsetTop + targetNodeEl.offsetHeight / 2;
                        const line = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'line');
                        line.setAttribute('x1', x1); line.setAttribute('y1', y1);
                        line.setAttribute('x2', x2); line.setAttribute('y2', y2);
                        line.classList.add('link'); svgContainer.appendChild(line);
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    }

    function displayFileContent(filename) {
        const file = fileSystem.files[filename];
        if (file) {
            const linkList = (file.links && file.links.length > 0) ? '\nLinks: ' + file.links.join(', ') : '\nLinks: None';
            const tagList = (file.tags && file.tags.length > 0) ? '\nTags: ' + file.tags.join(', ') : '\nTags: None';
            // Using alert for simplicity - replace with a modal/panel for better UX
            alert(`--- File: ${filename} ---\nFolder: ${file.folderPath}${tagList}${linkList}\n\n--- Content ---\n${file.content}`);
        } else { alert(`File "${filename}" not found.`); }
    }


    function updateTagFilters() {
        const tagContainer = document.getElementById('tagFilters');
        const currentActive = activeTagFilter;
        tagContainer.innerHTML = ''; // Clear

        const allTags = Array.from(new Set(Object.values(fileSystem.files).flatMap(f => f.tags || []))).sort();

        const allButton = document.createElement('button');
        allButton.textContent = 'All Notes'; allButton.className = 'tag-filter-button';
        if (!currentActive) allButton.classList.add('active-tag');
        allButton.onclick = () => setActiveTagFilter(null);
        tagContainer.appendChild(allButton);

        allTags.forEach(tag => {
            const button = document.createElement('button');
            button.textContent = tag; button.className = 'tag-filter-button';
            if (tag === currentActive) button.classList.add('active-tag');
            button.onclick = () => setActiveTagFilter(tag);
            tagContainer.appendChild(button);
        });
    }

    function setActiveTagFilter(tag) { activeTagFilter = tag; renderGraph(); updateTagFilters(); }
    function saveData() { try { localStorage.setItem('kettlekornFileSystem', JSON.stringify(fileSystem)); } catch (e) { console.error("Save Error:", e); alert("Could not save data."); }}

    // --- Drag-and-Drop Positioning ---
    const graphView = document.getElementById('graphView');
    graphView.ondragover = (e) => e.preventDefault(); // Allow drop
    graphView.ondrop = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const filename = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
        const nodeElement = graphView.querySelector(`.node[data-id="${CSS.escape(filename)}"]`);
        if (fileSystem.files[filename] && nodeElement) {
            const rect = graphView.getBoundingClientRect();
            let newX = e.clientX - rect.left - (nodeElement.offsetWidth / 2);
            let newY = e.clientY - rect.top - (nodeElement.offsetHeight / 2);
            newX = Math.max(5, Math.min(newX, graphView.clientWidth - nodeElement.offsetWidth - 5));
            newY = Math.max(5, Math.min(newY, graphView.clientHeight - nodeElement.offsetHeight - 5));
            fileSystem.files[filename].x = newX; fileSystem.files[filename].y = newY;
            nodeElement.style.left = `${newX}px`; nodeElement.style.top = `${newY}px`;
            renderGraph(); // Re-render links
            saveFileSystem();
        }
    };

    // --- Initial Load ---
    loadInitialData();
    renderAll(); // Initial full render

  </script>
</body>
</html>
```
